## Context

### Original Request
Build an AI overlay for NinjaTrader where an AI agent constantly watches the trading chart, providing real-time feedback. The AI has access to databases with news, GEX levels, options data, and MBO data. **Research Findings**:
- `transparent-overlay` v2.7.2: Layered windows, 60 FPS, click-through, thread-safe, Windows-native
- `python-mss` v10.2.0: 30-60 FPS screen capture, ~16ms per capture, GDI-based
- Claude Vision (Opus 4.7): Native high-res support, ~2765 tokens per 1920x1080 screenshot, $0.014/image
- Claude Sonnet 4.6: ~1568 tokens per screenshot, $0.0047/image, faster
- Existing DataBridgeServer on TCP 127.0.0.1:9200 streams NDJSON from NT8
- Existing FastAPI on port 8765 with WebSocket /ws/live broadcasting signals, scores, bars
- GEXGammaOverlay.cs proves SharpDX overlay pattern inside NT8
- Similar projects: ScreenSage, Proxeen (14-step adaptive pipeline), Screen Vision

### Metis Review
**Identified Gaps** (addressed):
- **Rate limiting / cost control**: Added token budget tracking and adaptive screenshot frequency
- **Context window management**: Rolling context with compression at token threshold
- **Stale data handling**: Each data source gets freshness tracking + staleness warnings
- **MAD level extraction reliability**: Vision model confidence scoring on level extraction; fallback to previous known levels
- **Overlay focus stealing**: transparent-overlay click-through mode + WS_EX_TRANSPARENT
- **Graceful degradation**: System continues with available data if any single source fails
- **Session boundaries**: RTH-aware (auto-pause during non-trading hours)

---

### Definition of Done
- [ ] Overlay sidebar renders on NT8 window with running text narrative
- [ ] Claude receives structured data + periodic screenshots and generates commentary
- [ ] MAD levels are identified from screenshots and referenced in trade calls
- [ ] All 5 new data sources connected and feeding context
- [ ] Trade recommendations include entry, stop, target, confidence, and MAD level reference
- [ ] System runs stable for 1+ hour during RTH without crashes or memory leaks
- [ ] Integration tests pass for all data adapters and screenshot pipeline

### Must Have
- MAD levels as primary framework in ALL AI analysis
- Running narrative that updates at least every 30 seconds
- Signal synthesis referencing 44-signal engine output + GEX + Kronos
- Screenshot-based chart analysis at configurable intervals (default 30s)
- Economic calendar event countdown ("FOMC in 22 min ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â reduce size")
- Graceful degradation when any data source is unavailable
- Token budget tracking to control Claude API costs
- RTH-aware operation (auto-pause outside trading hours)
- Click-through overlay (doesn't steal NT8 focus)

### Must NOT Have (Guardrails)
- NO autonomous trade execution ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â advisory only, trader decides
- NO TTS/voice in V1
- NO modification of existing DEEP6 signal engines ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â read-only consumer
- NO direct trading API calls from the copilot
- NO storing API keys in code ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â all via .env
- NO blocking the NT8 UI ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â overlay must be non-intrusive
- NO unbounded Claude API spending ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â hard token budget per hour

## Verification Strategy (MANDATORY)

> **ZERO HUMAN INTERVENTION** ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ALL verification is agent-executed. No exceptions.

### Test Decision
- **Infrastructure exists**: YES (pytest + existing test patterns in tests/)
- **Automated tests**: Integration tests for data plumbing; live QA for AI quality
- **Framework**: pytest (matching existing project patterns)
- **No TDD**: AI output quality can't be unit-tested; integration + live QA instead

### QA Policy
Every task MUST include agent-executed QA scenarios.
Evidence saved to `.sisyphus/evidence/task-{N}-{scenario-slug}.{ext}`.

- **Data adapters**: Use Bash (curl/Python REPL) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â call adapter, assert response shape + fields
- **Screenshot pipeline**: Use Bash ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â capture screenshot, verify file exists + dimensions + format
- **Overlay UI**: Use screenshot comparison ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â launch overlay, capture, verify rendering
- **Claude integration**: Use Bash (Python REPL) ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â send test prompt, verify streaming response structure
- **End-to-end**: Use Bash ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â launch copilot, verify overlay appears, verify narrative updates

  **Acceptance Criteria**:
  - [ ] `from deep6.copilot import CopilotConfig` imports without error
  - [ ] `python -m deep6.copilot --help` shows usage
  - [ ] All type definitions have proper type hints
  - [ ] Config loads from .env file
  - [ ] `pytest tests/copilot/` runs (even if no tests yet, no import errors)

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Package imports cleanly
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: deep6/copilot/ package exists
    Steps:
      1. Run: python -c "from deep6.copilot.config import CopilotConfig; print(CopilotConfig())"
      2. Assert output contains default values (screenshot_interval_sec=30, overlay_side='right')
      3. Run: python -c "from deep6.copilot.types import TradeCall, MADLevel, MarketNarrative; print('OK')"
      4. Assert output is 'OK'
    Expected Result: Both commands exit 0, no ImportError
    Failure Indicators: ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError, Pydantic validation error
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-1-package-imports.txt

  Scenario: Entry point responds to --help
    Tool: Bash
    Preconditions: __main__.py exists
    Steps:
      1. Run: python -m deep6.copilot --help
      2. Assert output contains "--dry-run", "--test-overlay", "--config"
    Expected Result: Exit 0, help text printed
    Failure Indicators: Exit non-zero, missing flags
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-1-entry-help.txt
  ```

  - [ ] `ScreenCapture.find_nt8_window()` returns valid coordinates when NT8 is running

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Capture screenshot when NT8 is running
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: NinjaTrader 8 is running with a chart open
    Steps:
      1. Run: python -c "from deep6.copilot.vision import ScreenCapture; from deep6.copilot.config import CopilotConfig; sc = ScreenCapture(CopilotConfig()); img = sc.capture(); print(f'Size: {len(img)} bytes'); assert len(img) > 10000"
      2. Assert output shows Size > 10000 bytes (valid PNG)
    Expected Result: PNG bytes captured, file > 10KB
    Failure Indicators: Size 0, win32gui error, mss GDI error
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-2-capture-screenshot.txt

  Scenario: Graceful handling when NT8 not running
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: NinjaTrader 8 is NOT running
    Steps:
      1. Run: python -c "from deep6.copilot.vision import ScreenCapture; from deep6.copilot.config import CopilotConfig; sc = ScreenCapture(CopilotConfig()); result = sc.find_nt8_window(); print(f'Result: {result}')"
      2. Assert no crash, returns None or raises documented exception
    Expected Result: Returns None, logs warning, no crash
    Failure Indicators: Unhandled exception, crash
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-2-no-nt8-graceful.txt
  ```

    - Polling interval: every 5 minutes (events don't change frequently)
    - Cache events locally; re-fetch on schedule

  ```
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-3-calendar-offline.txt
  ```

  - [ ] `analyze_chart()` returns ChartAnalysis with parsed MAD levels

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Extract MAD levels from chart screenshot
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in .env, NT8 running with madlevels.com indicator visible
    Steps:
      1. Capture screenshot via ScreenCapture
      2. Pass to VisionAnalyzer.analyze_chart()
      3. Assert mad_levels is non-empty list
      4. Assert each level has price (number), label (string), type (support/resistance)
      5. Assert confidence > 0.3
    Expected Result: List of MAD levels with prices matching what's visible on chart
    Failure Indicators: Empty mad_levels, confidence < 0.3, garbled prices
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-10-mad-levels.txt

  Scenario: Frame differencing skips identical screenshots
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: VisionAnalyzer initialized
  ```

    - Color scheme: dark background (rgba(20, 20, 30, 220)), green for bullish, red for bearish, amber for warnings
    - Click-through: `WS_EX_TRANSPARENT` flag so clicks pass through to NT8
    - Auto-reposition: if NT8 window moves, sidebar follows
    - Smooth text scrolling for narrative section

  - transparent-overlay rendering: `draw_text()`, `draw_rectangle()`, `set_render_callback()`, `start()`
  - Research finding: Double-buffered, Numba-accelerated, 60 FPS capable, thread-safe render loop

  **Acceptance Criteria**:
  - [ ] Overlay window appears docked to NT8 (right side)
  - [ ] Click-through works (clicking on overlay passes to NT8)
  - [ ] All sections render: header, trade call, narrative, countdown, status
  - [ ] `update_narrative()` causes smooth text update
  - [ ] Overlay follows NT8 when window moves
  - [ ] No focus stealing from NT8

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Overlay renders and docks to NT8
    Tool: Bash + screenshot
    Preconditions: NT8 running with chart open
    Steps:
      1. Launch overlay: python -c "from deep6.copilot.overlay import CopilotOverlay; from deep6.copilot.config import CopilotConfig; o = CopilotOverlay(CopilotConfig()); o.start(); o.update_narrative('Test narrative text'); import time; time.sleep(5)"
      2. Take screenshot of desktop showing NT8 + overlay
      3. Assert overlay is visible on right side of NT8
      4. Assert overlay shows "Test narrative text"
    Expected Result: Sidebar panel visible, docked to NT8, text displayed
    Failure Indicators: Overlay not visible, wrong position, focus stolen
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-11-overlay-render.png

  Scenario: Click-through to NT8
    Tool: Manual verification via screenshot comparison
    Preconditions: Overlay running on top of NT8
    Steps:
      1. Click on the overlay area
      2. Verify click passes through to NT8 chart underneath
      3. NT8 chart should respond (e.g., scroll, zoom)
    Expected Result: NT8 receives clicks through the overlay
    Failure Indicators: Overlay intercepts clicks, NT8 unresponsive under overlay
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-11-clickthrough.txt
  ```

  - Pre-commit: `pytest tests/copilot/`

  - DataBridge NDJSON format: `{"type": "trade"|"depth"|"bar", ...}` on TCP 9200

  **Acceptance Criteria**:
  - [ ] Connects to DataBridge (TCP 9200) and receives NDJSON events
  - [ ] Connects to FastAPI WebSocket (/ws/live) and receives signal/score messages
  - [ ] `get_latest_score()` returns valid ScorerResult
  - [ ] `get_latest_signals()` returns list of active signals
  - [ ] Auto-reconnects on connection drop

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Connect to live data infrastructure
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: DEEP6 engine running (DataBridge on 9200, API on 8765)
    Steps:
      1. Run: python -c "import asyncio; from deep6.copilot.bridge_client import CopilotBridgeClient; c = CopilotBridgeClient(); asyncio.run(c.connect()); score = c.get_latest_score(); print(f'Score: {score}')"
      2. Assert score is not None
    Expected Result: Valid ScorerResult from live engine
    Failure Indicators: Connection refused, None score, parse error
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-12-bridge-connect.txt

  Scenario: Graceful reconnection
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: Bridge client connected
    Steps:
      1. Connect successfully
      2. Simulate disconnect (stop DataBridge briefly)
      3. Restart DataBridge
      4. Assert client auto-reconnects within 30s
    Expected Result: Auto-reconnection without manual intervention
    Failure Indicators: Client stays disconnected, crash on reconnect
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-12-reconnect.txt
  ```

  - Files: `deep6/copilot/bridge_client.py`
  - Pre-commit: `pytest tests/copilot/`

  **QA Scenarios**:

  ```
  Scenario: Budget tracking and enforcement
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: CopilotConfig with token_budget_per_hour=1000
    Steps:
      1. Create tracker with small budget (1000 tokens/hour)
      2. Record 800 tokens usage
      3. Assert is_within_budget() == True, remaining = 200
      4. Record 300 more tokens
      5. Assert is_within_budget() == False
    Expected Result: Budget correctly tracks and enforces limits
    Failure Indicators: Wrong remaining count, no enforcement
    Evidence: .sisyphus/evidence/task-13-budget.txt
  ```

  ```
  Scenario: Generate trade call from high-confluence setup
    Tool: Bash (Python REPL)
    Preconditions: Mock context with confluence score 82, absorption + delta + auction signals, MAD S1 nearby

      1. Build mock MarketContext with score=82, direction=LONG
  ```

## Commit Strategy

| Wave | Commit | Message | Files | Pre-commit |
|------|--------|---------|-------|------------|
| 1 | After all Wave 1 tasks | `feat(copilot): scaffold package + data adapters` | deep6/copilot/*, tests/copilot/* | `pytest tests/copilot/` |
| 2 | After all Wave 2 tasks | `feat(copilot): Claude brain + vision + overlay + bridge client` | deep6/copilot/* | `pytest tests/copilot/` |
| 3 | After all Wave 3 tasks | `feat(copilot): narrative engine + trade calls + session manager` | deep6/copilot/* | `pytest tests/copilot/` |
| 4 | After all Wave 4 tasks | `test(copilot): integration tests + e2e smoke test` | tests/copilot/*, docs/ | `pytest tests/copilot/` |

## Success Criteria

### Verification Commands
```bash
pytest tests/copilot/ -v  # Expected: all tests pass
python -m deep6.copilot --dry-run  # Expected: starts, connects data sources, exits cleanly
python -m deep6.copilot --test-overlay  # Expected: overlay window appears docked to NT8
```

### Final Checklist
- [ ] All "Must Have" present (MAD levels, narrative, signals, calendar, etc.)
- [ ] All "Must NOT Have" absent (no auto-execution, no TTS, no signal engine mods)
- [ ] Integration tests pass
- [ ] Overlay renders without stealing NT8 focus
- [ ] Claude API costs tracked and within budget
- [ ] System stable for 1+ hour during RTH
- [ ] MAD levels identified and referenced in trade recommendations
